公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開
曼群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專
業生產各類氣動器材的集團 ， 公司主要從事
生產氣動控制元件 、 氣動執行原件 、 氣源處
理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售等業
務，以 「AirTAC」 自有品牌銷售於大中華地
區，中國市場佔營收 95％，台灣佔 2％、
海外佔 3％ ， 現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動
元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC 在中國市佔
率排名第一，達 33％。

主要客戶：電子業等 
主要競爭對手：SMC、Festo

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

亞德客-KY (1590 TT) 
獲利依舊在成長軌道上

永豐觀點 
自動化行業景氣持續回溫，伴隨公司市占率提升或推出新品，2026、2027 年獲利
依舊在成長軌道上。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議：理由包括：(1) 氣動元件 2026、2027 年仍處於上升週期，預估公
司營收皆將保持雙位數成長，此得益於中國政府政策激勵與公司市占率持續提升，
(2) 2026 年線軌市場景氣逐漸回溫，預計年底時公司稼動率可達五成，(3) 電控與
電動缸產品開始少量貢獻營收，公司將陸續推出新產品，開始累積客戶關係。評價
方面，公司本益比區間 20-40 倍，自動化行業景氣持續回溫，伴隨公司市占率提
升或推出新品 ，2026、2027 年獲利在成長軌道上 ， 維持買進建議 ， 目標價維持1972
元（27X 2027EPS）。

ESG 評析 
亞德客企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

亞德客為中國第二大氣動元件廠商：亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開曼
群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集團， 公司主要從
事生產氣動控制元件、 氣動執行原件、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售
等業務，以「AirTAC」自有品牌銷售於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 95％，台灣
佔 2％、海外佔3％，現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。 
2026、2027 年仍處於景氣上升週期，2026 年全年營收將成長 20%以上，2027
年亦有雙位數成長：過去氣動元件 2 年一個上升週期，2 年一個下降週期，本波
2025 年開始進入上升週期，趨勢較溫和，預計成長可超過三年。2022-2024 年行
業雙位數衰退，2025 低個位數成長、預估 2026 將高個位數成長，而 2027 年中
位數成長。公司規格持續擴增、市占率提升，公司增長比行業多 10%，公司對下
半年與明年成長的預期來自於客戶回饋、中國政府政策支持 （包括十五五計畫，除
製造業產業升級外，養老與醫護亦有著墨） ，對未來自動化有激勵作用。行業應用
別當中 ， 電子業電子行業前八月營收成長20%， 電子業中25%-30%與智慧手機有
關，主要客人在產品上市前 3-6 個月拉貨，預估客戶一般規格新手機明年上半年
上市，故公司七月與八月手機成長超過兩成，可延續至第四季，下半年電子行業也
維持 20%成長；電池行業中國政府有新政策，公司營收開始逐月下降，但年成長
仍有三成，預估明年該行業仍會成長，因客戶在海外擴充產能；汽車行業公司市占
率僅 10%-20%，市占率持續提升下，明年雙位數成長；傳統行業如食品、包裝、
工具機等受惠於政府補貼政策 ， 客戶積極擴充自動化產能，下半年仍維持雙位數成
長；太陽能行業，有機會衰退減少或轉為正增長；半導體受惠於中國國產化政策，
過去每月營收 500-600 萬元，目前1300-1400 萬元，2027 年新規格上市，4Q26
可讓客戶開始認證。 
線軌市場逐漸回溫 ， 預計年底時稼動率可達五成 ：線軌 2025、2026 年出貨量增長
20%，今年市場螺桿大幅調漲價格，線軌穩定價格，公司持續提升出貨量，目前稼
動率 40%，預估年底時可達 50%，屆時毛利率可達 30%，稼動率 80%時，毛利率
40%。公司預估今年營收可達 6.5-7 億元人民幣，規劃十年後年營收可達 30 億元
人民幣。 
跨入電控與電動缸領域，已開始貢獻營收：電控系統中國市場規模 1000 億元人民
幣，但有很多不同系統，公司會挑市場規模大的產品跨入，該產品資本支出低但毛
利率高。過去幾年利用五個系列產品在去年時營收達 4 億元人民幣，主要是搭配
氣缸銷售傳感器，公司自行設計功能佳、成本低的晶片，今年將再推出3 個系列，
公司預估未來 10 年公司有機會做到 30 億元人民幣年營收。電動缸產品目前公司
積極開發 servo motor、driver，主要搭配氣動元件銷售，2028-2029 有機會直接
應用在終端產品。目前市場規模 100 億元人民幣，隨著機器人市場興起規模會再
擴大 ， 中國servo motor 產品品質穩定 、 價格很低 ， 不排除公司直接買小型motor，
規劃 2027 年下半年上市小型電動缸。 
財務預估：3Q26 公司在大陸與台灣均進行明顯的調薪，將影響該季營業利益率，
預估將低於 2Q26，預估該季 EPS 為 15.23 元；去年第四季基期較高，不過預估今
年第四季營收仍將維持正成長，公司維持全年營收成長 20%以上，營業利益率維
持 34%的指引。預估 2026 年合併營收為 437.58 億元(+27.46％YoY)，稅後淨利
118.43 億元(+40.99％YoY)，EPS 為 59.22 元。預估 2027 年合併營收為 526.51
億元(+20.32％YoY)，稅後淨利 143.59 億元(+21.24％YoY)，EPS 為 71.79 元。

營運現況與分析

## Document 060: 260914_gs_optical-tour

Source PDF: all report pdf/260914_gs_optical-tour.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1. Optical module demand outlook: Companies we visited remain positive on 
optical module demand, driven by AI server racks ramp up, optical module adoption 
expansion (from scale out to scale up / across), and specification upgrade. On global 
market demand on 800G and 1.6T, demand could be at 130m to 200m; for the China 
market, overall demand could also grow at mid double digits to 50m+ units, with the 
majority 800G and 400G, and 1.6T emerging. Nevertheless, companies we visited 
with also mentioned that tight raw material supply (e.g. DSP , lasers, PCB) could 
continue to cap shipments in 2027. The positive tone echoes our positive view on 
global demand, on which we raised 800G and above optical module demand by 39% 
/ 36% in 2027E / 2028E to 144m / 171m (report link). Supply chain positive tone 
reflects further upside to our estimates. 
2. Optical module competition: Companies we visited see pricing competition 
remaining healthy, with 800G optical module pricing decline within single digits in 
2027, and 1.6T optical module price to sustain at US$700+. Managements highlight 
that optical module is not a pure assembly but with multiple SKUs and require 
suppliers’ strong R&D and timely development as compared to telecom, the product 
cycle could shorten to 1-2 years in datacom vs. 5 years in telecom. Supply chain 
management and mass production capability are also important to win orders, 
especially under the current tight supply situation and clients’ requirements of 
diversifying production sites under geopolitical tension. Innolight, as the global 
leader works with supply chain closely, including R&D collaboration, invests directly 
in their suppliers, and pre-payment and long-term agreements to secure a

### Page 2

sustainable supply through cycles.
3. Laser supply: Chinese suppliers are ramping up capacity and capability in 200G EML,
supporting the 1.6T optical module ramp up in 2027. Nevertheless, shipment also
depends on other raw material supply (e.g. DSP), and customers penetration.
Everbright‘s laser capacity prepared for 2027 is EML slightly higher than CW laser, and
within the EML majority is for 100G, with the remaining for 200G; Dongshan Precision

highlights their 200G EML is already in mass production, while volume is still small
currently given the tight supply of DSP; nevertheless, the company also secured 10m
supply of DSP in 2027, supporting their lasers and optical modules shipment ramp up.
VPEC, global leading InP epiwafer supplier, also sees improving InP substrate supply, with
steady export permission from China government, more customers bringing InP
substrate, and growing second to fourth suppliers outside of China. VPEC’s MOCVD is

also in expansion, from current 62 MOCVD to 69 MOCVD by 2Q27, and planning a new
production site in 2027.
4. CPO timeline: As a new technology, CPO will naturally take time and could face
challenges in the path of commercialization. For example, laser suppliers see thermal
challenges for 300mW / 400mW CW lasers in ELS (external laser source), and is working
on 100mW+ with PA for NPO first. Optical module suppliers also see strong 3.2T NPO
optical engine demand across China and overseas markets. Nevertheless, equipment

suppliers remain the earliest beneficiary in CPO, with the strong testing and coupling
demand reflecting in equipment suppliers’ revenues in mid 2026. For example,
Robotechnik 2Q26 revenues were up 172% QoQ, or 141% higher than our estimates. As
we highlighted in Sep (report link), we estimate CPO switch at 10k / 92k / 131k units in
2026E-28E.
5. AI spending sustainability: Management highlighted that AI is a revolution, and we
are at an early stage of infrastructure spending and application development. It will be a

long cycle for the coming 10 to 20 years, and some of their customers are planning
beyond 2030. The demand for the coming 2 to 5 years remains very strong and
management highlighted their industry enjoys short return on investment, which is
around 1 to 2 years. Management highlighted the AI spending is not only building more
AI data centers, but also includes technology migration, such as making power
consumption more efficient, making data transmission faster, etc., driving the supply

chain growth. Compared to the internet bubble in the 2000s, management sees much
more transparency between end-customers (demand) and supply chain (supply), as end
customers are involved more in supply chain product development and capacity
investment, reducing the information gap between demand and supply, and reducing
the chance of double booking. Management noted that currently every module they
produce is being deployed in data centers, reflecting real demand.

6. Photonics IC scaling up: AI drives data transmission speed and bandwidth
requirement, leading to strong demand on Photonics IC, requesting it to catch up
Electrical IC automated production and testing ecosystem in 1 to 2 years, while EIC /
semiconductors has been developed for decades. The strong demand brings
opportunities to tool suppliers, across coupling and testing, which are necessary tools to
accelerate PIC production. Robotechnik / FiconTEC describes the number of tools they

target to ship in the coming 1 year would be over 50% of what they’ve shipped in the
past 25 years, reflecting the strong demand and orders visibility.
14 September 2026   2
Goldman SachsFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

7. Testing - CPO earliest beneficiary: As a new technology, CPO naturally takes time
and faces challenges in the path of commercialization; however, we identify testing
equipment suppliers as the earliest beneficiaries, considering the expensive final module
means multiple testing during the production process is necessary, given the earlier a
failure is found, the lower cost a failure is. As a result, companies we visited continue to
see Insertion 2 (double-sided wafer-level testing) as necessary, and Robotechnik /

FiconTEC launched the new generation in 3Q26, which fully complied with the global
leading foundry’s communication infrastructure.
8. Testing - shortening time with KGD (know good die) at early stage: To facilitate
photonics IC scaling up, not only are more tools needed but better tools, requiring
suppliers’ strong R&D and execution, delivering continuous specification upgrade.
Robotechnik / FiconTEC, as global leaders in PIC testing, aim to shorten the wafer-level

testing time by 50-60% next year, and target 4x faster die-level testing next year.
Enlitech (7728.TWO, Not Covered) offers NightJar, a hyperspectral image system,
revealing AWG (arrayed waveguide grating) optical loss pathways with absolute optical
power mapping, allowing customers to KGD at Insertion 1 (PIC wafer testing) or Insertion
0 (PIC wafer early defect screening), saving failure costs.
9. Coupling - automated production becoming necessary in high-speed connection:

AI drives optical module towards high speed and high density / bandwidth, driving
demand on automated production. GMT (4573.TWO, not covered) showcased dual FA
active alignment equipment for 1.6T optical module, which could do the coupling for
both Tx and Rx simultaneously, saving 50% of coupling time compared to traditional
equipment. FitTech (6706.TW, not covered) has showcased an optical engine assembly
system, featuring passive / active alignment and customized development of coupling

algorithms, which could be used in multiple products: high-speed optical modules, ELS,
OCS, NPO, CPO, SiPh and optical chiplets. ASMPT has showcased MEGA multi-chip
bonding solution for complex multi-chip and photonics packaging applications,
combining high-precision placement with adhesive dispensing, UV fixation and 3D
inspection in one platform.
10. FAU & Laser specification upgrade: With optical networking expanding from scale
out to scale up, FAU and laser specification is also migrating. FOCI‘s FAU in 2026 to be

mainly 40 channels (starting in 3Q26) for 3.2T, followed by 80 channels (within 2026) for
6.4T, and 100 channels under R&D, per management. The company’s FAU enjoys several
customers across fabless, foundry, laser suppliers, etc. FOCI Aug revenues accelerated to
+20% MoM (vs. +9% MoM in Jul), reflecting the ramp up of high-end FAU. VPEC‘s
optoelectronics revenues enjoy strong growth on the back of strong CW lasers demand
and InP substrate supply getting better. Its InP epiwafer for CW lasers is also migrating

from 70mW / 100mW (scale out) to above 100mW (scale up, NPO) and 300mW /
400mW (scale up, CPO), along with expansion from CW lasers to 100G / 200G EML,
supporting its growth ahead.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Zhongji Innolight Co.
Valuation: Our 12-m TP for Innolight A-share of Rmb2,645 is based on 35.6x 2027E P/E,
and our 12-m TP for Innolight H-share of HK$3,267 is based on a H-A premium of 13%.
We derive our target P/E multiple from the networking peers’ trading P/E vs.
forward-year fundamentals (NI YoY and OPM). Compared to Innolight’s historical trading
range, the target multiple reflects our view of its leading market position in high-speed

connections and product-mix upgrade toward SiPh optical modules, driving GM
expansion. We are Buy rated.
Key downside risks: 1) slower-/worse-than-expected 800G+ optical modules demand;
2) slower/worse-than-expected new optical devices ramp pace; 3)
faster-than-expected market share normalizing in 800G+ optical modules; 4)
geopolitical risks; and 5) worsening component supply situation to constrain shipment
growth.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC

Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is
based on a 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics
supply chain.
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - RoboTechnik
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of Rmb796 is based on a 20.7x 2031E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 11.3%. Our target multiple is derived from the
correlation between consumer electronic peers’ PEG&M (2026E P/E to 2027E NI YoY and
OPM), with RoboTechnik’s 2032E net income at 2% YoY and 2032E OPM at 32%.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition; lower-than-expected

optical connection end demand; worse-than-expected photovoltaic market downcycle.
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of NT$833 is based on a 15.4x 2030E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 12.6%. Our target multiple is derived from peers’
correlation between forward-year trading P/E and earnings growth.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition, technology migration
trends in optical networking, and slower-than-expected capacity ramp-up.

Price target methodology and risks - ASMPT
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral rated on ASMPT with a 12-month target price
of HK$177, which is based on a 24.9x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E).
Our target P/E is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and
OPM.
Key upside/downside risks: 1) customers’ faster-/slower-than-expected adoption of
Advanced Packaging tools; 2) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand from

automotive customers; and 3) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for traditional
IC packaging and SMT equipment.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 068: 260914_ubs_parade

### Page 1

ab 14 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Parade Technologies
2026 Taiwan Summit: Near-term outlook
impacted by softer NB demand
Q326 revenue tracking toward lower-end of guidance
Parade attended our 2026 Taiwan Summit on Sep 14th. August sales of NT$1,327 mn
was down 2% and flat YoY. QTD sales only reached 57% of UBSe and Street estimates,
and Parade now expects its Q326 sales could come in at the lower-end of its US$139–
153 mn guidance range, likely due to softer NB demand. Parade's GM declined by 0.1

pp QoQ to 40.2% in Q226 on OSAT price hike, as the Taiwanese OSATs have allocated
more capacity to AI-related products. Parade noted it has been adding new OSAT
partners in Korea, China, and Malaysia, while also adjusting its ASPs in Q326. It noted its
Q326F GM should come in within its 40-44% guidance range, but we believe the GM
expansion will be more moderate given the cost headwinds. Q326F OPEX guidance is
reiterated at US$33-36 mn with full year OPEX growth to be within 5% YoY, and

effective tax rate for 2026F is guided to remain slightly above 10%. It has not provided a
specific outlook for Q426 but acknowledge the challenging outlook for the laptop
market. This is consistent with our recent checks within the NB display supply chain.
ASIC for CPU chipset to tape out soon for H227 mass production
Parade highlighted its deep expertise in high-speed connectivity and proprietary IP,
which enables it to deliver customized ASIC-like solutions to its customers. While its

initial focus was  display applications, it is now expanding  into CPU chipset. It is on-track
to tape out two chipsets for its CPU  customer, targeting  different PCIe channel
configurations. Comparing with its peers solution on 28nm node, Parade believes its
product  developed on 12 nm node  offers superior power efficiency. Parade noted the
CPU chipsets are  on track for mass production by H227, although we believe ASMedia's
solution will start to ramp from mid-2027 and will split the market share with Parade.

Exploring additional growth drivers for 2027
Parade also highlighted its ongoing effort to diversify away from traditional NB display  IC
business with several initiatives expected to contribute growth from 2027F. It sees good
revenue opportunity for  integrated touch solutions (TED/TTED), especially after the
potential adoption of touch display by  its standard-plus customer. For ACC cables, it will
supply redriver  with switch function, which should be a new growth engine in 2027F as

it believes copper and optical will co-exist in the next several years. Its USB4 retimer also
has good market share at non-Intel platform and management expects the penetration
to exceed  50% in 2027 vs 40%+ in 2026. Parade also noted its automotive business
should see strong sales growth in 2027, although we estimate overall contribution for
this segment remains at low-single-digit  % of total revenue.
Valuation: Rate NEUTRAL with NT$610 PT
We have a NEUTRAL rating on Parade with NT$610 PT,  based on 14.5x forward PE.

Company Description
Founded in 2005, Parade is a mixed-signal IC fabless design house headquartered in San Jose,
CA, specialising in high-speed interface and display solutions used in PCs, servers, consumer
electronics and display panels. Parade's display solutions (55% of 2024 revenue) include

embedded display port timing controllers (eDP TCON), source drivers and touch controllers.
Parade's high-speed interface solutions (45% of 2025 revenue) include signal conditioners
for USB, PCIe, SATA, etc, as well as protocol converters and USB controllers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for Parade is based on target PE multiple.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly

changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over IP rights.
For Parade, we believe key risks include: 1) Demand dynamics in PCs/notebooks, TVs, servers,
etc., as any pickup or slowdown in these end-markets would directly impact Parade's growth
and new product rollouts. 2) The length of the qualification cycle or pace of adoption by
clients could be a key upside/downside risk to our thesis. 3) Potential new entrants in Apple's

IT display timing controllers could be a downside risk to our growth estimates. 4) Fiercer price
competition by global and Asian peers may pose downside risk to our growth and GM
forecasts. 5) Foundry and assembly cost changes could present upside/downside risks to our
GM estimates.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over

the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

16 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
BizLink Holding
UBS T aiwan Summit 2026 key takeaways:
margins improve as HPC sales build
Positive outlook amid market volatilities
Bizlink's share price has been volatile amid gross margin weakness in Q126 and a
conservative read-through from Credo's earnings call on AEC products. Bizlink's
management remains positive about  its order momentum in HPC products, with data
cable shipments picking up, and power cable shipments  growing alongside rack

shipments and the higher content value of Vera Rubin. After the deal's completion, the
consolidation of Interplex should further expand the HPC sales contribution and drive
further margin expansion. Meanwhile, Bizlink has also identified opportunities in optical
connections through various partnerships. Earnings contributions from XFS are growing
robustly, though they remain a small proportion of the total. ALC and other CPO
revenue might not  contribute to earnings growth until H227, at the earliest.

Expect stronger HPC sales growth and margin expansion
Bizlink attributes the MoM sales decline in August to the summer holidays at its
European factories, which account for around one-third of capacity  and mainly supply
automation and industrial products. Management highlighted the historical patterns of
MoM improvements in September. Management remains positive about HPC and
capital equipment sales growth in the remainder of 2026. Data cables shipments have

recovered meaningfully since June. Power cables are growing rapidly with shipments of
Rubin servers, which have a much higher content value per rack than Blackwell. Further
upside could come from shipment of stand alone power side cars. The consolidation of
Interplex would serve as an additional growth driver after the deal is closed before end
of 2026. A higher sales mix from HPC could also drive gross margin expansion.
Engaging in various partnerships to capture optic interconnect opportunities

Management outlined several potential opportunities in optical connections: Credo's
ALC and other Micro-LED consortium products. These products provide mid-to-short
distance optical interconnection solutions. Credo plans to hold a demonstration in
October 2026, and Bizlink is the exclusive supplier. On CPO, Bizlink performs  fibre
assembly and supplies MPO connectors. It collaborates with FAU companies, including
Senko, and is working on solutions for shuffle boxes.

Valuation: Buy, price target at NT$3,617
We have a Buy rating with price target of NT$3,617, based on 30x 2027E PE.

Company Description
Founded in 1996, BizLink is headquartered in Fremont, California. BizLink provides
interconnect solutions, including connectors, harnesses and cables for a wide range of
industries, such as industrials, automotive, IT & data communications, and electrical

appliances. BizLink has 34 production facilities located in Asia, America and Europe.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a PE multiple.
The key company-specific risks are: 1) a global economic slowdown impacting end-demand;
2) a slower-than-expected change in the product mix leading to slower-than-expected
gross margin expansion; and 3) a rise in raw material prices.
The key industry-specific risks are: unexpected changes in the underlying global macro

environment, currency and interest rate movement and commodity price inflation.

Options and futures are not suitable for all investors, and trading in these instruments is considered risky and may be appropriate only for sophisticated investors. Prior to

buying or selling an option, and for the complete risks relating to options, you must receive a copy of "Characteristics and Risks of Standardized Options". You may read the
document at  https://www.theocc.com/about/publications/character-risks.jsp  or ask your financial advisor for a copy.
Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
CCL –  Doosan announces
capacity expansion in Korea and
China
What’s new? According to Business Korea, Doosan (000150.KS, not covered)
announced a KRW970B CCL capacity expansion on Sep 17:
• KRW420B in Korea to add optical module CCL lines, and
• KRW550B in China to build a new plant for AI accelerator and network
switch CCL.
Spending is phased through 2028, with the new lines coming online sequentially

from 2H28.
Our thoughts:  Our checks suggest this investment could add ~2,000K sheets per
month of CCL capacity at Doosan, equivalent to ~2% of global CCL capacity in 2028,
on our estimates. We view the investment as further evidence of robust demand for
high-end CCL and do not believe it undermines our thesis of continued high-end CCL
scarcity (see our CCL initiation report). As such, we see a positive read-across for
EMC, TUC, and Shengyi. We remain OW on EMC and TUC, while maintaining our EW

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Wiwynn (6669.TW): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion; 
Aug strong beat; TP up to NT$3,777; Buy
21 September 2026 | 6:21AM HKT
Wiwynn delivered strong Aug revenues, which were 27% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general servers demand from US leading CSPs, and its major 
customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up. We expect sequential revenues growth 
to continue through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +46% / 
+22% QoQ, driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across 
air-cooled and liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on 
agentic AI, (3) new GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 
across NVIDIA and AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI 
servers. Maintain Buy with TP raised to NT$3,777 (21.5x 2027E P/E vs. +32% EPS YoY 
in 2027-28E).

Ongoing capacity expansion: As we highlighted in Aug (report link), Wiwynn 
announced a 2H26 capex budget of US$942m, implying 2026 capex could be up to 
NT$40bn (US$1.3bn). On Sep 16, the company announced equipment procurement 
of US$16m (NT$512m), reflecting the strong AI demand and the company’s 
commitments on capacity expansion. We estimate the company’s capex at NT$44bn 
in 2026E, and to stay at a similar high level through 2028E, supporting its production 
sites diversification across the US, Mexico, and Malaysia, AI servers expansion from 
ASIC to GPU-powered, and products expansion across different ASICs and GPUs, 
along with further customers penetration. 
Earnings revision: We factor in August revenues, and change our net income by +7% 
/ +5% / +3% in 2026-28E. The change is mainly on revenues: we raise 2026-28E 
revenues by 8% / 7% / 5%, reflecting the strong beat of Aug revenues driven by

growing AI demand, which is also driving its general servers growth. Our EPS are reduced 
as we update the share count, reflecting the company’s stock split that started from 
Sept 2026. The mathematical effect on our EPS is around 66%.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our 2027E 
target P/E increases slightly to 21.5x (vs. 20x previously), which is still derived from the 
peers’ updated correlation of forward trading P/E and EPS growth. Accordingly, our 12M 
TP increases by 13% to NT$3,777.00 (vs. NT$3,352.55 previously). Our target P/E of 
21.5x is above the company’s historical avg.+1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on Wiwynn’s leading position in ASIC AI server ODM along with product 
expansion (from ASIC to GPU AI servers and general server) and customers expansion. 
Maintain Buy.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$3,777. Our target price is 
based on 21.5x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

### Page 2

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Strategy and positioning: Management launched PhotonLink as an architecture-
agnostic integrated optics platform covering CPO, NPO, and chip-to-chip optical
connections. It spans the full optical chain: Generating the light, shaping and
guiding it, and detecting it at the far end. Management discussed that its
customers asked it to supply complete assemblies rather than individual
components as stitching together lasers, fiber and passive optics is difficult. It

argued that no other company holds the full portfolio in-house and that Coherent
can manufacture at datacenter scale. Management expects a mix of adoption
paths, with some customers going straight to CPO and others starting with NPO
and moving to CPO later.
Figure 1. COHR's Portfolio of Optical AI Networking Products
Source: COHR at ECOC
Customers and content: In a full-solution example (combining the ELS, the
assembly to the switch or XPU, and the assembly out to the faceplate),

management said Coherent content could reach up to $15k of content per
switch/processor chip needing 100T of I/O capacity at 200G optical lane. Coherent
counts more than 10 customer engagements each in CPO and NPO and more than
5 in chip-to-chip, with activity going up sharply over the past six months. For CPO,
management said it has an anchor customer with an LTA secured, which it
identified as NVDA. It also has an anchor customer and LTA for NPO, which it did

not name. Separately, management cited two LTAs for ultra-high-power CW lasers
with leading hyperscale or AI datacenter customers, also unnamed.

### Page 3

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Figure 2. PhontonLink Platform
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Timing: CPO for scale-out, tied to the NVIDIA agreement, begins ramping in the
December quarter (calendar Q4 2026). CPO for scale-up follows in the second half
of calendar 2027. NPO ramps in the second half of next calendar year, mainly for
scale-up. Chip-to-chip is further out, around 2029-2030.
Light generation: The ultra-high-power CW laser sits in an external laser source

that holds up to eight lasers, each feeding roughly four links. It starts ramping this
quarter, with significant volume next year, on the 6-inch InP platform in Sherman,
Texas. For NPO, a high-power buried-heterostructure CW laser is already shipping
in millions of units per month for pluggables. VCSEL arrays come in a conventional
version for ribbon fiber and a high-density 2D version that can be flip-chipped
directly to drivers and TIAs with backside lenses. Photodetectors run to 200G

today, with 400G in development.
Figure 3. UHP CW Laser Leader
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026

### Page 4

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Fab footprint: Sherman runs 6-inch InP and GaAs, Sweden runs 6-inch InP . Zurich
runs 6-inch GaAs and is adding 6-inch InP for high-power lasers, while Fremont
runs a 3-inch line. GaAs capacity has grown fourfold since the 6-inch move about
six years ago, with capacity for over one billion VCSEL arrays per year. InP capacity
doubled last year, a quarter ahead of schedule, and management plans to double it

again or more.
Figure 4. COHR Capacity Expansion
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Shape, guide, and receive: Coherent grows garnet in the United States and says it
supplies a significant fraction of the industry's garnet and isolators, with
customers signing LTAs for capacity. The ELS is built on the transceiver line and is
sampling now. The rest of the portfolio covers specialty fiber (polarization-
maintaining fiber production-ready, mode-matched fiber introduced at ECOC,

multi-core fiber), micro-lens arrays, V-grooves, fiber array units, a detachable
connector, circulators, WDM multiplexers, and fiber shuffles.
Silicon photonics and engines: Management discussed Coherent having an in-
house silicon photonics design team, uses foundries for wafers, and does assembly
and test in-house. It ships 200G-per-lane silicon photonics in transceivers today
and showed a 400G link at OFC with a post-deadline paper. It has a 6.4T silicon

photonics NPO module (32 lanes of 200G) and a VCSEL-based NPO with 2D
arrays, which management says achieves about 1.2 pJ/bit including the laser.
Architecture trade-off: Management said NPO is easier to deploy and service but
costs power and signal integrity versus CPO, and that a common NPO socket can
take silicon photonics or VCSELs. It positioned Coherent as an honest advisor
because it can compare all the options. Chip-to-chip is a growing topic, with WDM

and circulators important there.

### Page 5

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
5
Figure 5. COHR CPO Offerings
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Figure 6. COHR NPO Offerings
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Key takeaways from Citi discussion with management:
What is Coherent's competitive advantage in 3.2T, and will it participate?
Management said it has already demonstrated the building blocks for the 3.2T
generation, including 400G-per-lane EMLs and 400G silicon photonics, and
expects to participate just as it enables 1.6T today. Management showed these

demonstrations as early as two years ago.
What is the gross margin path, and what offsets mix pressure? Management
mentioned that the 42%+ target was deliberately open-ended and that it expects
to exceed it. It cautioned against expecting a step change over the next two to
three quarters. Fast transceiver growth is a mix headwind, offset by overhead
absorption, yield gains and factory consolidation. Management expects the 800G-
to-1.6T transition to be margin-accretive, with the cutover forecast around 2028.

### Page 6

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
6
OCS, shipping for two to three quarters, is the first of the new platforms to
generate revenue. OCS, CPO and multi-rail will contribute more to margins as mix
builds.
What are the challenges and economics of the 6-inch InP transition?
Management said it announced 6-inch capability in March 2024 after years of
work with substrate vendors that began before the AI boom. Output is expected to
be roughly 50/50 between 3-inch and 6-inch by the end of the quarter, with all

incremental capacity on 6-inch, reaching about 75% by the end of 2027. A 6-inch
die costs about half as much per yielded die, and each line delivers about four
times the capacity. Yields are better on modern tooling. Management cited a three-
to-four-year lead over competitors and secured substrate supply from five
suppliers across three continents. It also credited its 6-inch GaAs VCSEL line to a
large consumer-electronics customer that demanded the larger wafer size.

Will Coherent keep external InP suppliers? COHR only sources EMLs externally
and that it will keep some external supply for continuity on a critical component.
How does Coherent differ from Chinese laser suppliers? Management argued
that module assembly is easier to replicate but high-speed lasers are not. It said
there are no high-speed EMLs or broadband tunable lasers from China, and that
Chinese CW lasers top out around 70-100 mW. It sees only one InP laser

competitor worth naming and expects new Chinese module entrants to take share
from existing Chinese suppliers rather than from Coherent.
Does NPO vs CPO matter for content, and can low-power lasers serve NPO?
Management said it does not matter much, because the components and dollar
content are similar. Designs are custom and take 18 to 24 months to reach volume,
and management reiterated scale-up production revenue in the second half of next
year. Management rejected the view that NPO can use low-power lasers, arguing

that coupling several weak lasers loses light and defeats the power-efficiency
purpose.
What is multi-rail, and how is it progressing? Multi-rail addresses scale-across
links between data centers. Management said it delivers four times the capacity in
the same footprint at roughly 30% lower power per lane, enabled by a multi-chip
uncooled pump that it says has no comparable competitor. Small shipments are
underway and production shipments begin in the first half of next calendar year. It

sells at the component, module, line-card and system level, including white-label
to OEMs.

### Page 7

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Coherent Corp
Company description
COHR is a vertically integrated manufacturing company that develops
engineered materials and opto-electronic components for industrial
materials processing, communications, aerospace and defense, consumer
electronics, semiconductor capital equipment, life sciences and automotive
end markets. COHR produces a wide variety of application-specific photonic
and electronic materials and components, and deploys them in various forms,

including integration with advanced software. COHR's customer base
includes original equipment manufacturers, laser end users, system
integrators of high-power lasers, manufacturers of equipment and devices for
industrial, optical communications, consumer electronics, security and
monitoring applications, U.S. government prime contractors, and various U.S.
government agencies. COHR is a headquartered in Pennsylvania.
Investment strategy
We are Buy-rated on COHR. We believe that the stock's multiple will benefit

from the AI appeal while its fundamentals should expand on new
management's strong execution and focus on profitability.
Valuation
We value COHR at $420 or 30x forward P/E applied to FY2028 EPS of ~$16
discounted back. Our COHR valuation is nevertheless at a 10% discount to
the average forward P/E of AI optics peers to reflect a lower AI sales mix and
3-year EPS CAGR vs peers such as LITE.
Risks
Downside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) increased

competition on AI and SiC leads to limited market share growth and limited
revenue upside, 2) inability to achieve synergies and margin targets
established post acquisition of Finisar, and 3) prolonged economic recession
leads to slower 5G build-out and lower consumer demand driving revenue of
COHR down.
Upside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) gaining share in
AI and SiC market through new product offerings and first to market status

on larger wafer sizes, 2) quicker and stronger adoption of AI workloads,
electric and autonomous vehicles, and 3) quicker return of margins to pre-
Finisar acquisition levels.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

## Document 101: 260922_ubs_GUC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260922_ubs_GUC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 18
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 22 September 2026
Global Research
Global Unichip
Better wafer allocation and demand upside
driving H226-2027E earnings growth
Better wafter allocation driving H226-2027E CPU project ramps
We expect CPU projects to remain GUC's main revenue driver in 2026-27E and see
improved wafer allocation into 2027E. We now expect Google CPU volumes to ramp
from 1.3mn units in 2026E to ~4mn units in 2027E, supported by rising TPU shipments
(up 117% YoY in 2027E), a rising CPU-to-TPU ratio, and better wafer allocation from

TSMC. Although GUC's CPU projects carry lower GM, we believe they remain earnings
accretive given the operating leverage from limited incremental OPEX requirements. We
now expect 2026E revenue growth of 94% YoY to NT$66.3 bn (vs prior +90% YoY) and
2027E revenue to grow 146% YoY (with Google CPU contributing 67% of 2027E sales),
up from our prior estimate of 84% YoY. We also expect OPM to expand from 13.2% in
2026E to 13.9% in 2028E. Accordingly, we raise our 2027/28E EPS estimates by

29%/22% and lift our PT to NT$8,660 (from NT$6,700), based on 52x average 2027-
28E EPS. Our 2027/28E EPS estimates are 23%/14% above consensus.
Broadening CSP exposure supports medium-term growth
Beyond the Google CPU ramp, we also see improving demand from Microsoft's Maia
platform, where GUC supplies CPU and the Maia 200 accelerator. Although Maia 300 is
shifting to another supplier, we see extended runway for Maia 200, as well as continued
CPU shipments into 2027E. For the next-gen Maia 400, we believe this remains in the

bidding stage, with GUC maintaining a competitive position over peers. GUC also
secured a 2nm ASIC project from Meta in Q226 for a turnkey revenue ramp from mid-
2028E. These CPU and ASIC projects, along with GUC's strong execution track record,
provide attractive opportunities to engage with top CSPs on accelerator opportunities.
Nevertheless, we do not expect meaningful revenue contribution before 2028.
Automotive to see more significant contribution from mid-2027E

We believe automotive will be another key growth driver for GUC over the medium
term, initially supported by a Chinese EV customer from late-2026, followed by a US EV
maker in H227E. We estimate automotive will contribute 2%/13% of total revenue in
2026E/27E, respectively, increasing further to 18% in 2028E. As GUC offers higher
value-add in the automotive projects vs. Google CPU, we believe they should support
GM and deliver a higher profit contribution relative to sales. In addition, revenue could

see further upside should these solutions expand into robotic applications.
Valuation: Retain BUY, raise PT to NT$8,660
Our NT$8,660 price target is based on 52x average 2027-28E EPS (vs prior 50x), vs
historical 17-84x range, supported by 56% CAGR in earnings over 2025-30E. We
reiterate our Buy rating, as GUC is well positioned for upside in CPU demand, ramp of
automotive projects, other ASICs and emerging optical interconnect opportunities.

### Page 2

Global Unichip UBS ResearchGlobal Unichip (3443.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can GUC further grow its AI and high-performance computing sales?
Yes, in our view, supported by multiple drivers. GUC has benefited from being part of TSMC's
ecosystem, with better access to TSMC's technology roadmap, especially on advanced nodes. It has
won several CPU/XPU projects from US hyperscalers and it is also working with leading EV makers on

next-generation autonomous driving ASICs. GUC aims to expand its tier-1 hyperscaler customer base
and we believe there is an opportunity for it to provide advanced packaging services, leveraging the
CoWoS capacity it secured, even though it is not directly involved in the ASIC design.
Q: Are GUC's projects on mature nodes (16nm and above) sustainable?
We think they are likely to remain stable. GUC's customers are sticky on 16nm and higher nodes.
Owing to its strong IP portfolio, which reduces design costs and improves manufacturing yield, these

mature nodes still contribute around 30% of GUC's sales in 2025. While we expect mature nodes to
remain accretive to margins – catering to repeat projects that require little R&D resources – the
growth is likely to be modest at 27% CAGR for 2025-28E vs 104% CAGR for advanced nodes.
Q: Will GUC's OPM recover in 2026-28?
We expect its OPM to show a slight improvement in 2026, as it further ramps the lower-GM Google
CPU project, and incurs a temporary increase in opex due to the new Turing centre. However, we

foresee a more significant improvement in OPM from 2027 as it ramps a higher GM autonomous
driving project from H227, growing  NRE contributions, and restocking of mature projects.
We reiterate our Buy rating on GUC and raise our 2026/27/28E EPS by 0.4%/29%/22%, respectively,
primarily reflecting higher sales contributions from Google's CPU projects. We raise our price target
from NT$6,700 to NT$8,660 based on 52x average 2027-28E EPS (vs a 17-84x historical range),

supported by a 2025-30E EPS CAGR of 56%. Potential catalysts include: 1) further TPU/CPU upside
from the Google project; 2) the continued ramp of Microsoft's AI CPU/ASIC chips and the potential to
win back future-gen projects; 3) upside on new 2nm project wins; 4) a faster ramp of autonomous EV
projects; and 5) advanced NRE opportunities with higher dollar content.
GUC's revenue remained muted in 2023-24, as it shifted its resources from Chinese to US customers

due to BIS export controls. The customer diversification effort began bearing fruit in H125, as GUC
recorded a notable uptick in the turnkey sales to US crypto customers, while the Google CPU ramp
since late Q425 appears sustainable. With the support of its major shareholder TSMC, which has
enabled better access to advanced technology, GUC continues to work with Microsoft, Tesla and
other hyperscalers on custom AI chipsets, which should underpin its  growth in the next few years.

GUC stock rallied 57% in the past three months vs 3% for the TAIEX, on Google TPU news in our
view, and is now trading at 55x 2027E EPS vs its historical 17-84x range. We believe the stock should
continue to re-rate on TPU upside, rising CPU/TPU ratio, multiple automotive project ramps, and
potential upside from wafer allocation. We expect  a 56% CAGR in earnings over 2025-30E and
foresee potential upward revisions in consensus earnings estimates being a catalyst.

GUC is an IC design service company founded in 1998. The company's business is to design chipsets
for its customers who do not have semiconductor capability. It is ~35% owned by TSMC.

Risk to the current share price is skewed (1.7:1) to the upside
GUC is trading at NT$7,615 (as of 21 September 2026).

UPSIDE (NT$12,000.00): Our upside scenario factors in stronger sales growth in 2027-
28E than we forecast in our base case, on the back of better demand for Google's TPU
and US/China automotive projects, more N3 wafer allocation by leading foundries, and
faster penetration of other hyperscalers, lifting 2027/28E sales by 170%/40% YoY. Our
upside OPM assumptions are 13.4%/14.1% for 2027/28E, 0.2ppt above our base case
owing to a limited increase in opex, supporting a 96% CAGR in earnings over 2026-28E.

Our upside scenario valuation of NT$12,000.00 implies a 76x 2027E PE.
BASE (NT$8,660.00): Our base case reflects our expectations of a solid business growth
outlook in 2027-28E, supported by the continued ramp of Google's CPU project, initial
ramp of automotive projects from Q426, and expansion into other hyperscalers. We
forecast 2027/28E sales to grow 146%/34% YoY, and OPM to expand to 13.2%/13.9%
from 12.7% in 2025 on operating leverage, despite its GM potentially normalising to

around 17.8% in 2028 (from 24.8% in 2025) on a higher proportion of sales from the
CPU project, resulting in earnings growth of 83% CAGR over 2026-28E. Our base-case
valuation of NT$8,660.00 implies a 52x 2027-28E average PE.
DOWNSIDE (NT$5,000.00): Our downside scenario factors in slower sales growth in
2027/28E than we forecast in our base case, due to softer growth in Google's CPU due to
a wafer capacity constraint and slower demand for mature process products, dampening

sales growth to 121%/26% YoY. Our downside OPM assumptions are 13.0%/13.7% for
2027/28E, 0.2pp below our base case, resulting in earnings growth decelerating to a
68% CAGR over 2026-28E. Our downside scenario valuation of NT$5,000.00 implies a
41x 2027E P/E.

Company Description
Global Unichip (GUC) is a leading IC design service company in Taiwan, founded in 1998. The
company's business is to design chipsets for customers that do not have IC design capability,
with the goal of reducing entry barriers and risks for chipset design and shortening the time

to market. The company's target to enable more IC design for start-ups and system
companies is complimentary with TSMC, which would benefit from a growing customer and
application base, in addition to the company's existing IC design and IDM customers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Global Unichip based on a PE methodology.
Downside risks include a slower-than-expected business recovery for mature nodes, lack of
new project wins, and wafer capacity constraints.

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 102: 260922_ubs_memory smartphone

Source PDF: all report pdf/260922_ubs_memory smartphone.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 22 September 2026
Global Research
Global I/O
Smartphones
Memory to continue to weigh in on
Smartphones in 2027
Smartphone unit Sell-In -3% YoY in '27E; initial '28E forecasts at +2% YoY
UBS 3Q26 APAC Tech Tour takeaways ( link) continue to point towards (1) further
downside for Android ex-Samsung smartphone unit builds into 2027, and (2) relative
resilience for Apple and Samsung. Memory procurement could turn out to be
incrementally more challenging for smartphone OEMs in 2027 than in 2026. Memory

makers continue to prioritise hyperscale and AI customers. Hence memory supply could
be even more of a bottleneck next year. As such, we further de-risk our '27E
smartphone unit Sell-In forecasts to 1.11bn (-3% YoY) from 1.16bn (+2% YoY)
prior and roll out initial '28E forecasts at 1.13bn (+2% YoY). We leave our '26E
forecasts unchanged at 1.14bn (-10% YoY).
Memory costs continue to go higher
We now estimate memory to account for 35% of the bill of material (BOM) costs of

flagship/premium smartphones and 56% of those of low-end smartphones by 4Q26E
(from 13%/29% in 4Q25), based on our current base case scenario of the DRAM/NAND
upcycle extending until at least 2Q28E/4Q27E. This is broadly consistent with Xiaomi's
commentary that memory now accounts for 40% of average smartphone BOM costs
and 50-60% of low-end BOM costs. We believe the current memory cycle will remain a
key challenge for OEMs as (1) increasing costs continue to pressure margins, and (2) ASP

hikes aiming to offset such pressures are likely to pose incremental downside risk to end
demand. Over the long term, we believe cost control will be more manageable for Apple
and, to a lesser extent, Samsung, given their premium product portfolio, while more
detrimental for others.
Apple & Samsung outperformance likely to continue
Global smartphone unit Sell-Through (source: Counterpoint) is now down 8.4% YoY Jul
'26 YTD. Within this, we note Apple (+8.9% YoY) and Samsung (-4.8% YoY; Sell-In up

YoY implies some channel inventory build) continue to outperform their Chinese
Android peers (Vivo/Xiaomi/Oppo/Transsion: -16.4%/-17.9%/-18.4%/-22.4% YoY).
With premium models like to fare better in this downturn, we believe Apple and, to a
lesser extent, Samsung will continue to outperform the broader market and gain
incremental share. iPhone suppliers continue to expect '27E to be a strong year for
Apple, with the iPhone Duo (c.10m units in first 12M) as well as the new launch

schedule likely to drive YoY unit growth. Samsung continues to believe it will be able to
grow unit and gain market share, though profitability will remain a concern.
Selective on smartphone plays; prefer memory/MLCC suppliers
Within the smartphone supply chain, we continue to prefer MediaTek and Samsung
(both APAC Key Call Buy) as well as ASE, Hon Hai, JCET, Micron, Murata, SEMCO,
SK Hynix and TSMC (all Buy). We remain cautious on LG Display, Maxscend, Xiaomi (all

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
The handset industry, as part of consumer electronics, is cyclically sensitive to the economy
and so upside and downside risks relate to macro drivers. Other downside risks include the
rapid loss of market share due to poor product timing, design or distribution. The supply
chain companies into the handset industry also operate in a highly cyclical market both from
macro concerns and inventory cycles within the supply chain. These companies also operate

in a highly competitive environment with product innovation and R&D investments critical to
success. Valuation methods across the sector include multiple analysis.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.
EXCEPTIONS AND SPECIAL CASES:UK and European Investment Fund ratings and definitions are: Buy: Positive on factors
such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

本次報告：論壇

欣興 (3037 TT) 
ABF 漲幅優於預期，獲利再度上修

永豐觀點 
3Q26 欣興 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，中線T-glass 玻纖布於 3Q27 前
供給吃緊，長線 ABF 供不應求至 2030 年，載板報價可望逐季推升 5-15%，獲利
持續高速成長，近期股價下跌反應檢調搜索利空，評價已修正至合理區間。 
 投資評價與建議 
欣興受惠 ABF 帶動產品組合轉佳，長線而言 ABF 於 2030 年前供給吃緊，載板報
價漲勢由成本推升改為需求拉動，3Q26 漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，且本波循環
公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 訂單，客戶遍及 GPU、ASIC 及 CPU 訂單，技術能
力僅次於龍頭廠 Ibiden， 研究部再度上調2026-2028 年稅後 EPS 至 23.57、42.71
及 65.08 元，投資建議買進，考量檢調風波對評價承壓，目標價維持 1575 元
(24X2028 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
欣興企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

2

欣興為國內 ABF 龍頭廠商：欣興為國內專業 IC 載板製造商，全球市佔率 18%僅
次於 Ibiden 以及 Samsung，2Q26 產品組合為 ABF(52%)、BT(9%)、HDI(27%)、
PCB(9%)、FPC(2%)及其它(1%)，其中 IC 載板毛利率優於公司平均水準。下游客
戶包含 Nokia、APPLE、Nvidia 以及 HTC，主要競爭對手為 Ibiden、Samsung、
Shinko、南電以及景碩。 
3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%， 暗示ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 ：欣興 8 月營收來到 177.46
億元(+9.2%MoM，+56.3%YoY)，除延續自 3 月以來月月創高紀錄外值得留意的
是月增及年增幅度亦同步擴大，整體 3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%亦優於原預期，暗
示 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點以上，研究部上調3Q26 營收至 525.83 億
元(+22.6%QoQ，+54.7%YoY)， 毛利率25.8%， 稅後淨利93.36 億元(-28.8%QoQ，
+325.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 5.68 元(原估 5.26 元)。 
CPU 生命週期展開使 ABF 需求更具延續性：過去 ABF 需求主要聚焦 AI Server 需
求，研究部原預估 2024-2025 年 ABF 供過於求 2%，2026 年亦難見反轉。受惠
AI Server 於 GPU 及 ASIC 需求帶動，另一方面 general server 回溫有望使 ABF
需求由高階產品延續至中階產品，Ibiden 預估相關高階 ABF 需求高速成長，加計
Production load 換算 2028 年面積需求較 2024 年增加 10.7 倍 ， 相較AI/ASIC 產
能於 2028 年擴充 1.8 倍供給仍低於需求。 
檢調搜索風波影響營運有限：日前欣興合江廠遭檢調搜索，股價亦應聲下跌，研究
部認為欣興合江廠位於中壢，主要生產 PCB 產品 2Q26 營收占比 9%，雖然後續
可能因產地標示不明影響 PCB 客戶關係 ， 惟與目前主力營運ABF 載板以及 HDI 並
無關聯。ABF 在 AI Server 需求推動下 2030 年以前供不應求，目前高階 GPU、
ASIC 產品僅 Ibiden(4062 JP)及欣興(3037 TT)具備相關供應能力，自研 CPU 則有
景碩(3189 TT) 緊追在後，考量產品認證需時 6-8 個月、關鍵壓合機台 lead time
亦 1-1.5 年，短期轉單不易。 
ABF 報價優於預期，上調 2026-2028 年營運：欣興 2026 年資本支出 537 億元，
其中 85%用於 ABF 除搭配客戶陸續啟動光復及楊梅 2 廠擴產，楊梅 3 廠可望於
4Q26-1Q27 動土，以土建時程 1.5-2 年推算 2029 年可望加入營運行列，未來 2
年資本支出亦可望維持 400 億以上水準。研究部考量 ABF 整體供給吃緊態勢延續
以及公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 產品，先進封裝客戶群布局廣泛，研究部上調

## Document 118: 3324雙鴻 永豐

Source PDF: all report pdf/3324雙鴻 永豐.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-15
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

個股聚焦

2026/9/15

財務資料 
 2025 
股東權益 (NT$百萬元) 11,085 
ROA (%) 11.95 
ROE (%) 25.66 
淨負債比率 (%) 56 
產業類別 散熱零組件 
投資建議 中立  
收盤價 6 個月目標價 
NT$ 1305.00 NT$

公司簡介 
雙鴻為台灣散熱模組廠商，主要提供風扇、
散熱片、熱導管等設計組合而成散熱工具，
裝置在筆記型電腦 、 桌上型電腦 、 伺服器及
智慧型手機等產品。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

黃璽銘 hsiming,huang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：公司拜訪

雙鴻 (3324 TT) 
水冷營收貢獻比重放大

永豐觀點 
水冷散熱營收貢獻大，持續關注獲利率表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持中立建議：2H26 受惠 GB300 系列水冷散熱相關產品出貨增加，以及
AWS Trainium3及輝達 Vera Rubin 世代相關水冷產品開始出貨，逐漸增添貢獻。
Trainium3 4Q26 主要為小量產階段，預期主要貢獻放量將落在 2027 年。雙鴻目
前正在驗證輝達 Vera Rubin 世代水冷板，預計最快 9 月底通過驗證，待驗證完成
後進一步加入營收獲利貢獻預估。 
隨著 AI 伺服器散熱需求越來越高，不論 GPU、ASIC 散熱設計都越趨複雜，也加
大投資需求，對獲利率表現形成挑戰。水冷散熱供應者眾，目前水冷散熱零組件市
場以奇鋐及 Cooler Master 產能較大及佔據主要份額。雙鴻營運主要能受惠整體
水冷散熱滲透率、市場產值提升，帶動營收規模擴大及獲利金額成長。 預估雙鴻
2026 年、2027 年水冷散熱營收比重 59%、68%，稅後 EPS 56.93 元、79.87 元。
研究部維持中立建議。 
 近八季營收及 YoY 趨勢圖

### Page 2

2

雙鴻為台灣散熱模組廠商，主要提供風扇、散熱片、熱導管等設計組合而成散熱工
具，裝置在筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、伺服器及智慧型手機等產品。高速運算晶片
發熱量持續增加， 散熱管理重要性提升。 散熱管理技術過往以主被動混合式熱管理
方案(鰭片+風扇)為主 ， 近年主流設計為均熱板 、 熱導管及3DVC 的散熱解決方案 ，
伺服器應用客戶積極導入水冷式散熱解決方案，六大關鍵零組件包含 Cold Plate、
CDU(Cooling Distribution Units)、Rack、RDHx、Quick Connectors、Manifold。 
雙鴻生產基地位於泰國、中國廣州、昆山、重慶、合肥。台灣散熱模組廠主要競爭
對手為奇鋐、Cooler Master、台達電、超眾及力致等等。客戶包括國內外筆電品
牌大廠及台灣 ODM 代工廠。2Q26 產品組合：PC 17%，VGA 4%，Server 78%，
Others 1%。 
水冷營收比重持續提高：先前雙鴻公告 7 月自結獲利，單月營收 37.7 億元，稅前
淨利 8.3 億元，稅後淨利 6.0 億元，稅後 EPS 6.47 元，優於預期，主因 7 月認列
部份 6 月遞延營收。8 月份客戶拉貨波動，單月營收月減 16.6%，預期 9 月份將
會回升。3Q26 雙鴻 GB300 水冷板、Manifold 出貨增加，以及預期 9 月份 AWS 
Trainium3 及輝達 Vera Rubin 世代相關水冷產品開始出貨，逐漸增加貢獻，主要
貢獻將落在 4Q26。 
研究部推估雙鴻 3Q26、4Q26 水冷營收比重達 61％、63%。營收分別為 103 億、
109 億元。水冷營收比重增加，同時增強獲利率表現，預估毛利率分別為29.9%、
30.3%，稅後 EPS 16.77 元、17.14 元。 
認證輝達 Vera Rubin 水冷板中 ：雙鴻目前正在驗證輝達 Vera Rubin 世代水冷板 ，
預計最快 9 月底通過驗證。研究部認為短期雙鴻出貨 Vera Rubin 世代相關水冷產
品將先以 Manifold 為主，待驗證完成後進一步加入營收獲利貢獻預估。同時雙鴻
也有望延續進入 Vera Rubin Ultra 專案，待後續相關水冷產品開發。 
ASIC 方面，雙鴻將量產 AWS Trainium3 水冷相關產品包含水冷板、 Inner 
Manifold、Rack Manifold，惟 4Q26 主要為小量產階段，預期主要貢獻放量將落
在 2027 年。後續包含 Meta ASIC 及 CDU 產品都有望額外增添貢獻雙鴻水冷營
收。 
產能持續擴充 ， 主要受惠水冷市場產值擴大 ：公司積極拓展伺服器水冷散熱市場機
會，2026 年、2027 年持續增加伺服器水冷產品產能。目前水冷板月產能包含廣州
及泰國共 400k pcs， 後續視情況在泰國增加100k~200k pcs。Manifold 台灣 2k、
泰國 3k。隨著 AI 伺服器散熱需求越來越高，不論 GPU、ASIC 散熱設計都越趨複
雜，如無塵室等級要求提高，也加大投資需求，對獲利率表現都會形成挑戰。 
水冷散熱供應者眾，目前水冷散熱零組件市場以奇鋐及 Cooler Master 產能較大
及佔據主要份額。雙鴻營運主要能受惠整體水冷散熱滲透率、市場產值提升，帶動
營收規模擴大及獲利金額成長。預估雙鴻 2026 年、2027 年水冷散熱營收比重
59%、68%。2026 年營收 385 億元，年增 65%，毛利率 29.6%，營益率 17.8%，
稅後 EPS 56.93 元。2027 年營收 513 億元，年增33%，毛利率 18.6%，稅後 EPS 
79.87 元。

## Document 124: 3605宏致 宏遠

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保

1 
3605 宏致(ACES)

報告日期： 2026/09/21 研 究 員：洪瑞陽  (Ryan Hung) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 公司法說指引 3Q26 及 4Q26 AI 營收占比分別提升至 27% 及
30% ；本研究預估 2026 全年 AI 營收占比約 25.8% ，2027 年
進一步提高至 39.9% 。 
 高速連接器 2026 年出貨約 3,200 至 3,300 萬顆， 2027 年目標
增至 5,000 萬顆，並受惠 ASIC 平台滲透率提高及 PCIe Gen6
規格升級，帶動出貨量與單位價值同步成長。  
 高速傳輸線及 Bloom Energy 產品 2027 年營收均以成長 2 至
3 倍為目標；本研究預估 2027 年兩項產品營收分別為 6.77 億
元及 25 億元。

一、 AI 營收占比快速提升，算力與電力共同驅動  
宏致自 2026 年起將 AI 產品區分為算力與電力兩大產品線，算力
包括高速連接器及機內高速傳輸線，電力則以 Bloom Energy 相關
高功率及高壓電纜為主。 2Q26 AI 營收占比已達 20%， 其中高速連
接器、高速傳輸線及電力產品分別約占 14% 、1%及 5%。

公司預估 3Q26 及 4Q26 AI 營收占比分別提升至 27% 及 30%，2027
年隨 ASIC 平台、高速傳輸線及 Bloom Energy 訂單放量，整體 AI
營收可望較 2026 年成長約一倍。本研究預估 2026 及 2027 年 AI
營收分別為 34.92 億元及 69.75 億元 ， 占合併營收25.8% 及 39.9%。

二、高速連接器產能擴充 2027 年出貨目標 5,000 萬顆  
高速連接器 2026 年上半年出貨約 1,200 萬顆 ， 下半年預估約2,100
萬顆，全年約 3,200 至 3,300 萬顆。月產能於 9 月提升至 4KK，10
月完成 5KK 產能建置，可支應 2027 年上半年需求； 2027 年出貨
目標進一步增至 5,000 萬顆。

目前 ASIC 占算力產品營收超過 80% ，2027 年將受惠既有 CSP 客
戶內供應機種增加，以及另一家美系 CSP 新機種進入量產。產品
規格亦將由 PCIe Gen5 逐步轉向 Gen6 ，新一代產品單位價值較前
一代提高約 10% 至 15% 。公司預期 2027 年算力產品營收可成長
50% 以上。

三、高速線與 BE 產品  2Q26 開始貢獻，明年倍數成長  
機內高速傳輸線於 2Q26 正式量產，公司預估 2026 年營收占比約

2 至 3 倍，對應約 20 至 30 億元

四、預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 為 5.13 / 8.96 元 
2026 年受惠高速連接器產能及出貨量逐季提升、機內高速傳輸線
開始量產，以及 Bloom Energy 訂單集中於下半年放量，預估全年
營收 135.25 億元 ，YoY 增長 24.5%； 毛利率25.62%、營 業 利 益10.45
億元、營業利益率 7.73% ，稅後淨利 8.75 億元，稅後 EPS 約 5.13
元。

2027 年高速連接器出貨量增至 5,000 萬顆，並受惠 PCIe Gen6 帶
動 ASP 提升 ， 高速傳輸線及Bloom Energy 營收亦分別成長至 6.77
億元及 25 億元，預估全年營收 174.82 億元， YoY 增長 29.3% ；毛
利率提升至 26.86% ，營業利益率升至 10.94% ，稅後淨利 15.70 億
元，稅後 EPS 為 8.96 元。

五、投資建議：買進，目標價 180 元 
宏致成長動能已由傳統 NB 及消費性連接器，轉向 AI 高速連接
器、機內高速傳輸線及資料中心電力產品。 2027 年高速連接器將
受惠出貨量增加、 ASIC 主板滲透率提升及 PCIe Gen6 規格升級，
高速傳輸線亦將進入營收放量與良率改善階段， Bloom Energy 電
力產品則具有 2 至 3 倍成長空間。

本研究預估 2027 年 AI 營收 69.75 億元， YoY 增長約 100% ，占合
併營收 39.9%； 產品組合改善將帶動營業利益與稅後淨利分別年增
83.1% 及 79.4% 。考量 2027 年進入獲利加速期、 AI 營收比重接近
四成，且主要產品訂單及出貨能見度提高，給予 20 倍 2027 年預
估本益比，以稅後 EPS 8.96 元計算，目標價約 180 元，投資建議
維持買進。  
貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況 
一、公司簡介  
宏致成立於 1996 年，總部位於台灣桃園，專注於高階精密連接器
與連接線的研發及製造，全球員工超過 5,000 人，並於亞洲、美洲
及歐洲建構完整製造與服務網絡。公司具備從產品設計、模具、
沖壓、電鍍至組裝及測試的垂直整合能力，目前於全球六大製造

3 
地區設有 10 座生產工廠，業務服務據點遍布 11 國。  
公司主要分為三大事業群。連接器事業群以宏致為主，專注於高
速、高頻及高可靠性連接器，並具備模具、沖壓、電鍍及整合組
裝能力；連接線事業群產品涵蓋高速傳輸線、工業及電源線材，
以及車用線束；金屬沖壓事業群則提供金屬支架、電磁屏蔽罩、
伺服器機殼及機箱機櫃等精密結構件，並可延伸至 Level 3 機構組
裝。  
宏致早期以 NB 機內連接器為主要業務，逐步將應用領域由消費
性電子延伸至工業、汽車、網通及雲端資料中心。公司目前依終
端應用將產品區分為工業、網通、 AI、汽車及 NB／消費性電子五
大類，其中 AI 自 2026 年起獨立分類，並進一步拆分為算力與電
力兩大產品線。  
算力產品包括高速連接器及機內高速傳輸線，主要應用於 AI 伺服
器主板、加速卡、連接卡及其他功能性小板；電力產品則以 Bloom 
Energy 相關高功率、高壓電纜為主。隨 CSP 自研 ASIC 平台、高
速互連及資料中心電力需求放量，宏致營運重心正由傳統 NB 及
消費性連接器，逐步轉向 AI 算力與電力應用。本研究預估 AI 營
收占比將由 2025 年的約 10% ，提高至 2026 年的 25.8% 及 2027 年
的 39.9% 。

二、由周邊小板切入 ASIC 主板  高速連接器量價齊升  
AI 伺服器內部需透過高速連接器，完成 CPU 、GPU 、ASIC 加速
器、網卡、記憶體及儲存設備之間的資料傳輸。隨伺服器運算速
度及頻寬提高，高速連接器除須支援更高的傳輸速率外，使用數

量、規格複雜度及單位價值亦同步增加。目前宏致高速連接器以
MCIO 為最大宗，另供應板對板連接器、小型 Power 連接器及其
他高速介面產品，產品涵蓋伺服器內部不同主板及周邊小板的連
接需求。  
宏致於 2024 年開始切入 NVIDIA 平台相關高速連接器， 2024 至
2025 年主要供應主板周邊小板、加速卡及連接卡，當時單一 Tray
平均高速連接器用量約 10 顆，公司尚未切入 NVIDIA 主板。 2025
年底起，隨美系 CSP 自研 ASIC 平台進入量產，宏致由周邊小板
進一步切入完整主板專案 ， 單機高速連接器用量提高至約 25 至 30
顆， BOM Value 呈倍數增加。  
目前 ASIC 平台占公司算力產品營收超過 80%， 為高速連接器最主
要的成長來源。公司主要服務兩家美系 CSP ，其中一家既有客戶
內可供應的機種及平台持續增加，另一家客戶則預計於 2027 年增
加量產機種。 ASIC 滲透率提高並非僅指單一機種供應份額上升，
而是宏致能參與的客戶平台、量產機種及主板位置同步增加，使
高速連接器成長具備延續性。  
高速連接器 2026 年上半年出貨約 1,200 萬顆 ， 下半年預估約2,100
萬顆，全年約 3,200 至 3,300 萬顆。產能方面， 9 月月產能已提升
至 4KK，10 月完成 5KK 月產能建置 ， 可支應2027 年上半年需求。
本研究採 2026 年出貨 3,300 萬顆估算，預估高速連接器營收約
22.22 億元，占合併營收 16.4% 。 
2027 年公司高速連接器出貨目標進一步提高至 5,000 萬顆，相較
2026 年約成長五成 。 除出貨量增加外 ， 產品規格亦將由PCIe Gen5
逐步轉向 Gen6 ，2027 年 Gen6 可望成為主要規格。公司表示新一
代產品單位價值較前一代提升約 10% 至 15% ，反映高速傳輸速度
提升後，產品在材料、模具精度、訊號完整性及製造良率等方面
的要求同步增加。  
本研究預估 2027 年高速連接器營收增至 37.98 億元， YoY 增長
71% ，成長同時來自出貨量增加、 ASIC 主板滲透率提高及 PCIe 
Gen6 帶動 ASP 上升。高速連接器平均毛利率約 45%，明顯高於公
司平均，後續隨 5KK 月產能開出、稼動率提升及固定成本攤提增
加，將成為公司毛利率及營業利益率改善的主要來源。

三、機內高速連接線開始量產  成套供應提高單機價值  
當 PCIe 傳輸速度持續提升，電訊號在主板上傳輸較長距離時，容
易出現訊號衰減及傳輸品質下降。因此，部分高速訊號將不再完
全透過主板走線，而改由機內高速線材直接連接主板與周邊小
板。每條機內高速傳輸線基本上由兩個高速連接器搭配一條線
材，與公司既有高速連接器形成互補。

2026 年為機內高速連接線正式量產第一年，公司預估全年營收占
比約 2%。 本研究預估2026 年高速線營收約 2.71 億元 ， 其中2Q26、
3Q26 及 4Q26 分別約 0.34 億元、0.95 億元及 1.42 億元，呈現逐季
放量趨勢。 2027 年隨已認證專案進入量產及客戶平台增加，公司
預期絕對營收可成長 2 至 3 倍 ； 本研究採指引中值估算 ， 預估2027
年營收約 6.77 億元， YoY 增長 150% 。

高速線目前毛利率約 10% ，低於高速連接器，主因仍處於量產初
期，良率及製造效率尚未完全最佳化。公司預期 2027 年隨產量增
加及良率提高，毛利率可逐步改善至約 20% 。因此，高速線 2026
年對合併毛利率貢獻仍有限，但 2027 年將同時受惠營收規模擴大
及毛利率改善，成為高速連接器之外的第二項算力成長動能。

高速線的意義亦不僅限於單一產品營收。宏致原先主要供應標準
化高速連接器，切入客製化機內高速線後，可同時供應連接器與
線材，由單一零組件供應商逐步轉為高速互連成套供應商，有助
提高單一伺服器平台的供應價值、客戶合作深度及後續專案導入
機會。

四、 BE 產品逐步放量， 2027 年估營收 25 億元  
隨 AI 資料中心用電需求快速增加，電力供應已成為限制資料中心
建置速度的重要因素。相較等待傳統電網擴建及併網， Bloom

### Page 6

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

6 
Energy 透過標準化固態氧化物燃料電池模組，可於資料中心附近
建置分散式電源，並依用電需求增加模組數量，有助縮短資料中
心取得電力的時間。

Bloom Energy 的 Energy Box 大致可分為 Hot Box 高溫反應模組及
PCS 電力轉換系統。 Hot Box 負責電化學反應及發電，需要耐高溫
線材與 Hot Box Cable ；PCS 則負責直流電轉換 、 電力管理及Power 
System Control ，需要高壓電纜、功率電纜及控制訊號傳輸線。各
模組完成後，再透過 System Cable 與外部電力系統連接。

宏致供應的產品包括 Hot Box 耐高溫線材、 PCS 相關高壓及功率
電纜，以及電力管理與控制訊號傳輸線。公司並非全部直接出貨
予 Bloom Energy ，而 是 依Bloom Energy 指定 ， 將產品交付至台灣、
印度、韓國及越南等地的系統與模組供應商，再由相關供應商完
成模組組裝後交付 Bloom Energy 。

Bloom Energy 相關產品高度客製化，目前約有 596 個料號陸續進
入量產。不同 Energy Box 及客戶 Layout 所使用的線材數量、長度
與組合皆不相同，且 Bloom Energy 未提供完整 BOM，因此無法由
單一 Energy Box 組數或 GW 出貨目標，直接線性推算宏致營收。
應以訂單金額及公司營收指引作為主要估算依據。

宏致過去即為 Bloom Energy 既有供應商，但相關業務規模有限，
2025 年營收僅約 1 億多元。 2026 年隨 Bloom Energy 資料中心供
電專案進入放量期，相關營收快速增加，公司表示 2026 年全年營
收達 10 億元沒有問題，且出貨明顯集中於下半年。本研究同樣預
估 2026 年 Bloom Energy 營收約 10 億 元，其 中3Q26 及 4Q26 分別
約 3.10 億元及 4.66 億元。

公司目前手中訂單能見度已至 2027 年 6 至 8 月，2027 年內部營收
目標為 2026 年的 2 至 3 倍，對應約 20 至 30 億元。本研究採指引
中值估算，預估 2027 年 Bloom Energy 相關營收約 25 億元， YoY
增長 150% ，占合併營收約 14.3% ，成為宏致僅次於高速連接器的
AI 產品線。

Bloom Energy 相關產品毛利率約 20% ，未來隨規模增加及製造效
率改善，有機會提高至 20% 以上。其毛利率雖低於高速連接器，
但宏致菲律賓廠過去即具有施耐德及 Eaton 等電機與電源線纜產
品經驗，可直接沿用既有設備及 Know-how ，新增研發、資本支出
及 SG&A 投入相對有限，因此營業利益貢獻優於單純觀察毛利率
所呈現的水準。

五、產品組合轉變， AI 產品組合帶動獲利升級  
除 AI 產品外，宏致傳統業務包括工業、網通、汽車及 NB／消費
性電子。工業產品主要應用於 UPS 、工業設備、攝影機、馬達及
無人機，毛利率約 15% 至 20% ；網通產品涵蓋一般伺服器及網路
設備，毛利率約 30% 至 35% ；汽車產品主要為車用電子及訊號傳
輸，毛 利率 約15%；NB 及消費性產品則包括機內連接器、 DDR5 、
Type-C 及電競相關產品，毛利率約 20% 至 25% 。

工業及網通仍為公司相對穩定的基本盤。工業產品受惠 UPS 、攝
影機及無人機等需求，公司預期 2026 年維持成長；網通全年亦維
持成長，但下半年受到部分零組件缺料影響，短期出貨可能承壓。
汽車產品因公司近年投入資源相對有限，營收約維持持平。

NB 及消費性產品受到終端買氣疲弱 、 客戶長短料及材料成本上升

公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

8 
影響，傳統第三季旺季表現不如以往。公司預估 2026 年 NB 及消
費性營收較 2025 年衰退約 10%，2027 年則隨供應鏈及終端需求回
穩而成長約 5%。雖然傳統產品成長有限，但既有營收規模仍有助
吸收固定成本，並提供公司進行 AI 產品擴產與開發的基本盤。

本研究預估 2026 年工業、網通、汽車及 NB／消費性營收分別為
28.18 億元、18.69 億元、5.49 億元及 47.97 億元，傳統產品合計占
營收 74.2% ；2027 年四項傳統產品營收分別預估為 29.58 億元、
19.58 億元、 5.49 億元及 50.42 億元，整體維持低個位數成長，營
收增量主要仍由 AI 產品貢獻。

宏致三項 AI 產品的獲利結構並不相同。高速連接器毛利率約
45%， 是產品組合改善的主要來源；機內高速傳輸線目前毛利率約
10% ，2027 年可望隨良率提高改善至約 20% ；Bloom Energy 產品
毛利率約 20% ，但新增研發及營業費用較低，營業利益貢獻相對
較佳。

因此，宏致後續獲利改善並非單純來自 AI 營收占比提高，而是高
速連接器高毛利產品放量、高速線良率改善、 Bloom Energy 規模
經濟，以及營收成長帶動費用率下降共同推升。本研究預估 AI 營
收占比由 2026 年的 25.8% 提高至 2027 年的 39.9%， 帶動合併毛利
率由 25.62% 提升至 26.86% ，營業利益率則由 7.73% 提高至
10.94% ，顯示產品組合改善。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估 
一、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 為 5.13 元 
2Q26 營收 33.50 億元， QoQ 增長 18.7% ，主要受惠 AI 營收 QoQ
增長 70% ，以及工業、網通與消費性產品較 1Q 農曆年低基期回
升。2Q26 毛利率 25.64% ，較 1Q 提高 1.05 個百分點；營業費用率
隨營收規模放大而下降，帶動營業利益率由 1Q 的 5.23% 提升至
7.34% ，單季稅後 EPS 為 1.22 元。

展望下半年，高速連接器 9 月月產能提升至 4KK，10 月完成 5KK
產能建置；機內高速傳輸線自 2Q 開始量產後逐季增加， Bloom 
Energy 訂單亦明顯集中於下半年 。 本研究預估3Q26 AI 營收 10.06
億元，占營收 29.4% ，單季營收 34.17 億元、毛利率 25.68% 、營
業利益率 7.47% ，稅後 EPS 1.26 元。

4Q26 高速連接器出貨量預估提高至 1,208 萬顆， Bloom Energy 營
收增至 4.66 億元，機內高速傳輸線營收亦提升至 1.42 億元，帶動
AI 營收增加至 14.21 億元，占營收 36.1% 。預估 4Q26 營收 39.36
